##### What was that? Instagram's escape plan is Parental Alerts, Warner Bros. finally accepts the Paramount deal, Google reshuffles Search for the EU, Snap gets an award show, and the rest is...

This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at [Archive](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), it tracks everything, including stories that don’t mention you directly. It becomes a full-circle moment where you can measure the impact of your efforts while also [uncovering new creators](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to partner with.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VaNH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/d026eb5f-e052-4990-8ffb-4078962631ed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Instagram](https://substack.com/redirect/bbf2ddc5-0671-4067-ba6e-9a658dea10a4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to alert parents if teens search for self-harm and suicide content.
	+ Safety campaigners say Meta is "[passing the buck](https://substack.com/redirect/9a6e4305-faf3-4831-b10c-de363df9912c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)" with this new feature.
* Google might [reshuffle search results](https://substack.com/redirect/70b9f907-5f2a-4d1a-8d86-564375f00b94?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to try to dodge fines in the EU.
* The ‘[QuitGPT](https://substack.com/redirect/35850811-d20e-488d-a2bf-865decd2bd93?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ movement gains steam as [OpenAI’s Department of War deal](https://substack.com/redirect/002504ec-5956-474f-8d24-14c266771b33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has users saying ‘Cancel ChatGPT.’
* Following Instagram and TikTok, [Snapchat](https://substack.com/redirect/1d7f1808-b29c-4d42-a25d-cf7d3de52e4c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) announces ‘[The Snappys](https://substack.com/redirect/c666f4a9-819c-44ec-94d7-1a81fe48f743?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),’ its first-ever creator awards show.
* [Netflix](https://substack.com/redirect/7a0ecd6b-8ca5-45e5-a826-e3f1e049e38f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shockingly [bows out](https://substack.com/redirect/7f43065d-6620-4371-a58a-57ddd65bf07a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), leaving [Paramount](https://substack.com/redirect/bf656112-ee8e-4d7c-abdc-2512c4cf3318?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as the apparent Warner Bros. winner.
	+ CNN CEO urges employees to not “[jump to conclusions](https://substack.com/redirect/c3fae1d2-82d6-487b-8d3e-0f5c52edb45b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” about Paramount.
* [Reddit](https://substack.com/redirect/50b2ced3-3ab3-4451-aae4-95068edabc9e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) fined $20 million by UK for [not effectively checking](https://substack.com/redirect/96be631c-0367-4618-86c7-475ffc638c23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) users’ ages.

	+ [Anthropic](https://substack.com/redirect/98a2a91e-aeec-494f-9ef2-5eb0c77ead95?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) says it ‘[cannot in good conscience’](https://substack.com/redirect/55d12e4e-bbb1-4264-a5b9-2e863d648132?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) allow Pentagon to remove AI checks.
	+ US military [reportedly used](https://substack.com/redirect/ac1f89f1-5ad9-4a9f-a7c7-fc0374ef278a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Claude in Iran strikes despite Trump’s ban.
* Google pulls 115 Android apps tied to [ad fraud scheme](https://substack.com/redirect/9ddf5d32-d9f3-4d2d-bd66-ba1e3ffdd22d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) affecting 25M devices.
* [Mr. Clean](https://substack.com/redirect/d59080c0-1565-455d-ad1c-d9b11ec18d4a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the bald, broad-shouldered cleaning mascot, retires.
* [Pinterest search trends](https://substack.com/redirect/77e57843-c9c2-4492-8b27-d738ac5490f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reveal [push for](https://substack.com/redirect/dc5f283f-0890-4715-9edd-68968cfab5bd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) offline and experience-rich parenting.
* [Google Maps](https://substack.com/redirect/84658c13-7101-48b3-b987-720dd12538b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) wins access to South Korea — one of last countries where app does not work.
* Paramount revenue, streaming subscribers [inch up in Q4](https://substack.com/redirect/0d87bcce-9c1d-43c4-b074-11c933bab827?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), but losses widen on TV slump.
	+ HBO Max hits [131.6M subs](https://substack.com/redirect/67ab7599-deb7-4812-b341-f0b6e0cab40b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), NBA exit pinches [advertising](https://substack.com/redirect/294c1964-083b-4f0b-95ee-1f1b8723f2e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as WBD deal drama dominates Q4.
	+ California now the [biggest obstacle](https://substack.com/redirect/d1648957-e982-43f1-9772-0569dc8f687a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to Paramount's Warner Bros takeover.
* [Discord](https://substack.com/redirect/a9f2a4c6-e2c6-4399-b76b-ad6fe7d52157?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) postpones [global age verification](https://substack.com/redirect/9b19e17b-cfc5-45e4-aed9-c53884bed116?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to the second half of 2026 as [it considers](https://substack.com/redirect/3fd7c34d-ca99-44aa-a43c-952e3d72fbbc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) adding more verification options.
* [Stripe](https://substack.com/redirect/1863ac94-346f-4b9b-befd-26bb88eaa5ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) considers acquisition of all or parts of [PayPal](https://substack.com/redirect/ac94c721-f016-4b70-8114-9ec1c7868d29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Samsung’s [new Privacy Display](https://substack.com/redirect/e2b56ffd-7f53-497b-b096-60e62b440646?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) may not be Ultra [exclusive for long](https://substack.com/redirect/d8a693d5-fe0a-4a3b-9b9f-5c447cee452e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

##### Not sure where your brand is being mentioned? [Archive](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) tracks it for you.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!yPRT!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F94514ea2-8c57-49df-ae6e-883a5711d56d_6912x2910.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)There’s literally nothing worse than asking a big creator to resend content because you missed their Instagram Story. Except realizing they posted and didn’t tag you at all. If you’re sending product and relying on tags to track performance, you’re probably missing more than you think. [Archive](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) fixes that…

It detects tagged and untagged content 24/7 across Instagram, TikTok, and YouTube, because it watches video, listens to audio, and reads text, not just @mentions. Every post gets [pulled into one place](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and rolled up into campaign-level EMV alongside reach, engagement, and content volume.

So instead of chasing creators in your DMs or taking screenshots for proof, you can actually see [what your seeding campaigns](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are driving. If you want to scale creator programs without missing the posts that matter, this is it. And still, the question I get more than anything is always some variation of this: **How do I actually find creators who make sense for my brand without losing hours to the scroll?**

With [AI Creator Search](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), you can finally stop scrolling and start filtering by brand, criteria, and performance to find creators who actually fit. Apart from your gifting/seeding efforts, you can also discover creators who already love your brand, even if you haven’t worked with them yet.

> [Book the demo.](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Try it for two weeks. [Thank yourself later…](https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

* UK news giants form ‘[NATO for news](https://substack.com/redirect/540a0623-6b8c-45ad-a3f6-3d22a9a97ca4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ group to control [AI scraping](https://substack.com/redirect/abf759fe-1b17-494b-a0a0-7f7a038b6270?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Chatbots](https://substack.com/redirect/30f819ec-9df1-468c-a8ee-f676d8d6634b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are the new influencers brands must woo.
* This AI-generated podcast network publishes [11,000 episodes a day](https://substack.com/redirect/0120fcea-11d1-4fd8-b39b-f92d562017f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). It also ripped off media outlets.

	+ AI added ‘[basically zero](https://substack.com/redirect/f92b246a-4365-4b3c-9183-219b82258147?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to US economic growth last year, Goldman Sachs says.
* Despite 2025 revenue beats, [The Trade Desk’s stock price falls](https://substack.com/redirect/a3dd93f8-6cd9-4d30-8d44-1c9342b59c75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) sharply after earnings update.
* ‘[AI; didn’t read](https://substack.com/redirect/dbf5e773-355b-4b67-aad2-3a0524934f05?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: AI;DR is the new TL;DR.
* [WPP](https://substack.com/redirect/31c6df47-e54f-4dc3-890c-253a67d029a6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) abandons [holding company model](https://substack.com/redirect/e3778692-b085-4139-8c00-a202f9f52839?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in [major strategic overhaul](https://substack.com/redirect/3e6579c2-1ff8-429e-b6e0-924ce8082603?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

* As video podcasts boom, [SAG-AFTRA](https://substack.com/redirect/ce4c55da-b05d-43e3-9f76-8f357a604ce3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) looks to organize the industry.
	+ Sofia Franklyn is writing a memoir about the ‘[Call Her Daddy](https://substack.com/redirect/c9388b1d-cfb4-40a5-b1c5-f03e2bccc3d0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ fallout.

	+ Nearly [one in three Meta ads](https://substack.com/redirect/ab395ea9-3dc6-4a0b-8581-7ff002f4a463?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reportedly point to a scam, phishing or malware.
* Meta AI alignment director shares her [OpenClaw email-deletion nightmare](https://substack.com/redirect/bba38308-d30c-4df3-b8f6-3ad84fda8adf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): ‘I had to RUN to my Mac mini.’
* [I.R.S. tactics](https://substack.com/redirect/ac3dcd35-7930-4421-8229-eb4444a8100d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) against Meta open a new front in the corporate tax fight.
* [Facebook travel groups](https://substack.com/redirect/ce1e7b5a-daf5-4791-a837-690293f6a93f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) can be a gold mine—if you avoid the trolls.
* [Meta](https://substack.com/redirect/81c637eb-f1c1-4cb2-9e38-f03114cdad4a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) sues [advertisers in Brazil and China](https://substack.com/redirect/e0a953f7-ae6c-4a7c-945a-d700a5452246?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over ‘celeb bait’ scams
* WhatsApp might finally support [traditional passwords](https://substack.com/redirect/4b0c2fbc-5a8e-4ea8-b607-44c66adbb57b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in addition to 2FA.
	+ Promoted Channels and [ads in Status](https://substack.com/redirect/59a73082-95b8-4db0-b549-10b558c6f9d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are available globally now.
	+ WhatsApp is [finally working](https://substack.com/redirect/55977187-36fd-4635-9f00-63785fe8c049?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on scheduled messages.
* Setback for Meta as court adviser backs [EU regulators](https://substack.com/redirect/5fdce52f-38c3-45bd-ad3c-271c095e90ba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in data spat.
* Meta tests [standalone Vibes web app](https://substack.com/redirect/e22e046e-bd57-4ee8-aa5c-bac9cba183aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), new AI video creation tool.
* National Parent Teacher Association [breaks ties with Meta](https://substack.com/redirect/0a14693a-7a6c-4e5b-b7ba-6dea4463f6cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) amid child-safety trials.

* YouTube Shorts and Instagram Reels are [making you dumber](https://substack.com/redirect/3de6d486-c591-4816-b491-5c2a5b04809a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), according to science.
* YouTube partners with [Rakuten](https://substack.com/redirect/c6893255-9b7c-40d4-944b-4c0089d46a74?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to bring more shoppable videos to Japan.
* YouTube [adds](https://substack.com/redirect/dae34ab1-c455-45b8-9eba-dee7da4e7315?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) voice replies and AI-powered Shorts remixing.
* Kalshi fined [a MrBeast editor](https://substack.com/redirect/068d5b92-a59b-40e1-9e42-2904dd61b673?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for insider trading on markets related to the YouTube star.
* YouTuber sues [Runway AI](https://substack.com/redirect/5909c613-0b35-4930-a69c-89ac33d1b50c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in latest copyright class action over AI training.
* Phonk producer Slxughter reached [981m YouTube users](https://substack.com/redirect/10fc94b1-7603-48dc-9600-30e55aae928c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) last month.
* YouTube TV’s [generous offers](https://substack.com/redirect/bd67b5b7-fcd9-490a-8b4e-92d8c2eaa59e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) available to some subscribers.
* YouTube is ruining the [Subscriptions section](https://substack.com/redirect/f985fbd2-a070-42e8-b3c2-fac385764a63?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with a new test.
* HubSpot [acquires](https://substack.com/redirect/87fa403e-1d5d-4687-ad2b-7f151cc6998c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) YouTube-based media brand Starter Story.

* Google AI Overviews accelerates its presence across [nine industries](https://substack.com/redirect/80959d20-11c0-4580-a2c7-3e70c862a89b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

---

* Google [apologizes](https://substack.com/redirect/dff0aaf9-9e9f-4a28-840b-904a6ac5765d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after news alert about [BAFTA Film Awards](https://substack.com/redirect/21f18a7a-e430-4681-b19f-d242ded3873b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) debacle included the n-word.
* Google publishes new [Google Ads passkey](https://substack.com/redirect/e1d780b0-a16d-46d2-a296-086bc168e4b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) help doc.
* [Klaviyo](https://substack.com/redirect/e6af6aaf-5de0-4cfb-924b-2e16e277477a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and Google announce new strategic partnership.
* Google quantum-proofs [HTTPS by squeezing 15kB of data](https://substack.com/redirect/6728e906-5656-48c9-8782-283b3706c303?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into 700-byte space.
* Merchant Center becomes [a central video hub](https://substack.com/redirect/4193e921-16f5-4ba8-8dc0-d76a4250b704?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Google auto-imports content.
* Google strikes [multibillion-dollar AI chip deal](https://substack.com/redirect/8f43086a-5cea-40b1-962c-f4fe6a553859?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Meta, sharpening Nvidia rivalry.
* Google Ad Grants now lets [nonprofits optimize](https://substack.com/redirect/e4699664-c462-4f2c-a94b-dc5091a8bf19?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for shop visits.
* OpenAI and Google employees have [signed a petition](https://substack.com/redirect/72701834-b50f-44c2-b00c-205663055d98?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) opposing the military’s AI use.
* Google looks to tackle longstanding [RCS spam in India](https://substack.com/redirect/770fa1b0-afb4-4bcc-9844-b72eab404c3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) — but not alone.

> 🛼 Report Break: In 2015, AM/FM radio accounted for 75% of the time Americans spent with spoken-word audio sources.

> As of Q4 2025, 40% of time spent listening to [spoken-word is now spent with podcasts](https://substack.com/redirect/a2542047-7c52-47b8-b58c-5de9ee11da50?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and 39% of time is spent with AM/FM radio. Not only does radio not beat podcasts by a significant margin, it now trails the on-demand platform for spoken-word audio listening.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!J7DY!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Facd06a12-7508-4bfa-91b1-8c434a20fc84_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/5de23958-6957-445a-8245-f7eb11cd6a20?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Musk’s X](https://substack.com/redirect/60b59539-2aa4-4eb6-ae24-489494d3426a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches [topic filtering](https://substack.com/redirect/c1057c88-2db6-42ab-9701-4d5c9ff3ea82?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to help users customise their timeline/feed.
* xAI spent [$7M building wall](https://substack.com/redirect/d0dbfd19-9e60-4568-83af-0010b5113888?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that barely muffles annoying power plant noise.
* X will soon [make you disclose](https://substack.com/redirect/2ddd744a-aa62-4b24-82e5-ad2dcbae9a84?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) if your post [contains](https://substack.com/redirect/13d8650d-4e06-45b1-bd1b-9366eaaa5454?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) AI generated content.
* [FBI got Grok](https://substack.com/redirect/061c318d-0a92-4ab5-a8cc-118eda6464a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to hand over prompts used to create nonconsensual porn.
* X invites [advertisers to recycle creative](https://substack.com/redirect/3af65d22-22d6-42d9-9f10-5c47590564d7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from other platforms.
* SpaceX could [seek IPO valuation](https://substack.com/redirect/67486cb2-b652-4f25-bc6e-52ba679a3aeb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of over $1.75 trillion, Bloomberg says.
* X's Head of Product Nikita Bier says the [platform's search](https://substack.com/redirect/de81116d-ff9a-44f3-b245-1c5824c1d89b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) function is being rebuilt.
* X adds two aspect ratio options for [in-stream ads](https://substack.com/redirect/b7b8077a-1265-4c58-b22c-ea9b613e49a8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Musk has no proof OpenAI stole [xAI trade secrets](https://substack.com/redirect/9aaea60d-9955-438e-a5d4-0ed13c457b29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), judge rules, tossing lawsuit.
* xAI tests [Voice Cloning on Grok for iOS](https://substack.com/redirect/8746f06d-d34a-462c-b1df-4cfda9fbdfbd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with shareable voices.
* Musk’s [xAI and Pentagon](https://substack.com/redirect/87a0adc8-ff6e-4283-892d-51710cb9e1da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reach deal to use Grok in classified systems.

* Snapchat is testing [a new UI](https://substack.com/redirect/fe7b1a7a-65e0-44c3-b165-c41b85dfa7f8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Spotlight posts.

> 👺 Report Break: A majority of teens (59%) think [using AI to cheat is a regular occurrence](https://substack.com/redirect/5dd9e591-c06f-48b4-8345-f78c24551fea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at their school – happening at least somewhat often. Another 14% of teens say students at their school rarely or never cheat using AI chatbots. Still, some teens are unsure if this happens (15%).

> [![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Efgh!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9ede6c85-dd74-47a4-9592-b8161649c25b_930x626.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/30988d18-0e92-4837-92bf-440d9ce0b860?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

> More than half of teens say they have used chatbots to search for information (57%) or get help with schoolwork (54%). And 47% say they’ve done so for fun or entertainment.

* Sky Media brings outcomes ‘[certainty](https://substack.com/redirect/65b410ce-7218-4ed7-bc76-2ae5653ec449?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to addressable TV planning.
* Mission Andromeda introduces [Gong Enable](https://substack.com/redirect/0e2071a9-e763-4e6c-8cfc-b48151b5858e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), an AI-powered revenue enablement solution.
* Bedrock launches [Multi-Agent CTV OS](https://substack.com/redirect/9ed7805d-9d9c-48cf-9920-70be466bf845?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Pathfinder beta.
* OOH reports [highest-ever annual revenue](https://substack.com/redirect/308bf0bc-8b1a-4b00-a2b0-178b9ca98d77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2025.
	+ Revenue for year-end 2025 grew 2.6% year-on-year to £1.44bn, [beating ad revenue in 2024.](https://substack.com/redirect/8751e076-e5c8-4aec-accc-bb404abafd08?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Digital OOH grew 3.2%, and classic OOH was up 1.3%, with DOOH taking a 67% overall share of annual revenue.
* [Addressable TV](https://substack.com/redirect/122b6c70-e16a-4bce-b8f5-41c60524e7c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) delivers stronger campaign results with greater certainty.
* [SerpApi](https://substack.com/redirect/0c187bd5-14bf-4da0-b0ab-f715e288b7a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) moves to dismiss Google scraping lawsuit.
* WFA launches [guide to inclusive insights](https://substack.com/redirect/9bfdd736-a876-42f6-92c8-01e4761bc1a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to help strengthen consumer understanding.
* OUTFRONT Media and AdQuick [form](https://substack.com/redirect/8a8b261d-d260-498e-ba62-24088b2ccc8f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) exclusive commercial partnership.

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Archive</a><span>, it tracks everything, including stories that don’t mention you directly. It becomes a full-circle moment where you can measure the impact of your efforts while also </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">uncovering new creators</a><span> to partner with.</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money – you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0610d009-4285-437b-8549-b4a9054e167b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="728"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d026eb5f-e052-4990-8ffb-4078962631ed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":728,"bytes":50683,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!VaNH!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bbf2ddc5-0671-4067-ba6e-9a658dea10a4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram</a><span> to alert parents if teens search for self-harm and suicide content.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Safety campaigners say Meta is "</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9a6e4305-faf3-4831-b10c-de363df9912c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">passing the buck</a><span>" with this new feature.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google might </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/70b9f907-5f2a-4d1a-8d86-564375f00b94?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reshuffle search results</a><span> to try to dodge fines in the EU.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/35850811-d20e-488d-a2bf-865decd2bd93?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">QuitGPT</a><span>’ movement gains steam as </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/002504ec-5956-474f-8d24-14c266771b33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI’s Department of War deal</a><span> has users saying ‘Cancel ChatGPT.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Following Instagram and TikTok, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1d7f1808-b29c-4d42-a25d-cf7d3de52e4c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Snapchat</a><span> announces ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c666f4a9-819c-44ec-94d7-1a81fe48f743?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Snappys</a><span>,’ its first-ever creator awards show.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7a0ecd6b-8ca5-45e5-a826-e3f1e049e38f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Netflix</a><span> shockingly </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7f43065d-6620-4371-a58a-57ddd65bf07a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bows out</a><span>, leaving </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bf656112-ee8e-4d7c-abdc-2512c4cf3318?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Paramount</a><span> as the apparent Warner Bros. winner.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>CNN CEO urges employees to not “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c3fae1d2-82d6-487b-8d3e-0f5c52edb45b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">jump to conclusions</a><span>” about Paramount.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/50b2ced3-3ab3-4451-aae4-95068edabc9e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Reddit</a><span> fined $20 million by UK for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/96be631c-0367-4618-86c7-475ffc638c23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">not effectively checking</a><span> users’ ages.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0eb37d4f-fda6-45f2-8dee-8ec3c0c9e8ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":48232,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!zM4F!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Anthropic </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/26df3120-a8ab-4cca-a058-c2a64af4cd84?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">hits back</a><span> after US Military labels it a ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d56cd8d4-1ef7-4641-aeed-724cc5317fb2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">supply chain risk</a><span>.’</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/98a2a91e-aeec-494f-9ef2-5eb0c77ead95?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Anthropic</a><span> says it ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/55d12e4e-bbb1-4264-a5b9-2e863d648132?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">cannot in good conscience’</a><span> allow Pentagon to remove AI checks.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>US military </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ac1f89f1-5ad9-4a9f-a7c7-fc0374ef278a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reportedly used</a><span> Claude in Iran strikes despite Trump’s ban.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google pulls 115 Android apps tied to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9ddf5d32-d9f3-4d2d-bd66-ba1e3ffdd22d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ad fraud scheme</a><span> affecting 25M devices.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d59080c0-1565-455d-ad1c-d9b11ec18d4a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Mr. Clean</a><span>, the bald, broad-shouldered cleaning mascot, retires.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/77e57843-c9c2-4492-8b27-d738ac5490f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Pinterest search trends</a><span> reveal </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dc5f283f-0890-4715-9edd-68968cfab5bd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">push for</a><span> offline and experience-rich parenting.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/84658c13-7101-48b3-b987-720dd12538b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google Maps</a><span> wins access to South Korea — one of last countries where app does not work.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Paramount revenue, streaming subscribers </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0d87bcce-9c1d-43c4-b074-11c933bab827?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">inch up in Q4</a><span>, but losses widen on TV slump.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>HBO Max hits </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/67ab7599-deb7-4812-b341-f0b6e0cab40b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">131.6M subs</a><span>, NBA exit pinches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/294c1964-083b-4f0b-95ee-1f1b8723f2e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">advertising</a><span> as WBD deal drama dominates Q4.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>California now the</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d1648957-e982-43f1-9772-0569dc8f687a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> biggest obstacle</a><span> to Paramount's Warner Bros takeover.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a9f2a4c6-e2c6-4399-b76b-ad6fe7d52157?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Discord</a><span> postpones</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9b19e17b-cfc5-45e4-aed9-c53884bed116?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> global age verification</a><span> to the second half of 2026 as </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3fd7c34d-ca99-44aa-a43c-952e3d72fbbc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">it considers</a><span> adding more verification options.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1863ac94-346f-4b9b-befd-26bb88eaa5ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Stripe</a><span> considers acquisition of all or parts of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ac94c721-f016-4b70-8114-9ec1c7868d29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">PayPal</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Samsung’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e2b56ffd-7f53-497b-b096-60e62b440646?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new Privacy Display</a><span> may not be Ultra </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d8a693d5-fe0a-4a3b-9b9f-5c447cee452e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">exclusive for long</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Archive</a></p></blockquote>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>Not sure where your brand is being mentioned? </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">Archive</a><span> tracks it for you.</span>
</h3>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="6912"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/94514ea2-8c57-49df-ae6e-883a5711d56d\_6912x2910.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":2910,"width":6912,"resizeWidth":null,"bytes":3951152,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":"https://go.archive.ai/1d9fe9","belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/188778665?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1ef58e8e-c0c1-40bd-8b75-8c20e0eb51fb\_6912x3456.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="231.55381944444446" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!yPRT!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F94514ea2-8c57-49df-ae6e-883a5711d56d\_6912x2910.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>There’s literally nothing worse than asking a big creator to resend content because you missed their Instagram Story. Except realizing they posted and didn’t tag you at all. If you’re sending product and relying on tags to track performance, you’re probably missing more than you think. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Archive</a><span> fixes that…</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>It detects tagged and untagged content 24/7 across Instagram, TikTok, and YouTube, because it watches video, listens to audio, and reads text, not just @mentions. Every post gets </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">pulled into one place</a><span> and rolled up into campaign-level EMV alongside reach, engagement, and content volume.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>So instead of chasing creators in your DMs or taking screenshots for proof, you can actually see </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">what your seeding campaigns</a><span> are driving. If you want to scale creator programs without missing the posts that matter, this is it. And still, the question I get more than anything is always some variation of this: </span><strong>How do I actually find creators who make sense for my brand without losing hours to the scroll?</strong></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>With </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI Creator Search</a><span>, you can finally stop scrolling and start filtering by brand, criteria, and performance to find creators who actually fit. Apart from your gifting/seeding efforts, you can also discover creators who already love your brand, even if you haven’t worked with them yet.</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Book the demo.</a><span> Try it for two weeks. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d39b7f05-f293-43d7-8faa-b9c0dea45d3d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Thank yourself later…</a></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>UK news giants form ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/540a0623-6b8c-45ad-a3f6-3d22a9a97ca4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">NATO for news</a><span>’ group to control </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/abf759fe-1b17-494b-a0a0-7f7a038b6270?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI scraping</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/30f819ec-9df1-468c-a8ee-f676d8d6634b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Chatbots</a><span> are the new influencers brands must woo.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>This AI-generated podcast network publishes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0120fcea-11d1-4fd8-b39b-f92d562017f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">11,000 episodes a day</a><span>. It also ripped off media outlets.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Does Big Tech actually care about fighting </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d485bda1-a74c-4cb9-bb25-2593e40fed65?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI slop</a><span>?</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Block lays off </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1201eb75-52e5-4c76-8e27-2001679849b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">40% of workforce</a><span> as it goes all-in on AI tools.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AI added ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f92b246a-4365-4b3c-9183-219b82258147?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">basically zero</a><span>’ to US economic growth last year, Goldman Sachs says.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Despite 2025 revenue beats, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a3dd93f8-6cd9-4d30-8d44-1c9342b59c75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Trade Desk’s stock price falls</a><span> sharply after earnings update.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dbf5e773-355b-4b67-aad2-3a0524934f05?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI; didn’t read</a><span>’: AI;DR is the new TL;DR.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/31c6df47-e54f-4dc3-890c-253a67d029a6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">WPP</a><span> abandons </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e3778692-b085-4139-8c00-a202f9f52839?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">holding company model </a><span>in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3e6579c2-1ff8-429e-b6e0-924ce8082603?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">major strategic overhaul</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Salesforce chief dismisses ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/34a87e61-3ad3-44db-a184-90eaed77626e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">SaaS-pocalypse</a><span>’ fears of AI overtaking business software.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>As video podcasts boom, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ce4c55da-b05d-43e3-9f76-8f357a604ce3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">SAG-AFTRA</a><span> looks to organize the industry.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Sofia Franklyn is writing a memoir about the ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c9388b1d-cfb4-40a5-b1c5-f03e2bccc3d0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Call Her Daddy</a><span>’ fallout.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Data centers are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c2e61ab3-3323-4d37-99f4-8a7b569b75e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">racing to space</a><span> — and regulation can’t keep up.</span></p></li>

<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/58a2af37-eaa4-4192-a03b-1e55d82ddce6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI</a><span> announces </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f830e337-e653-43fb-93df-d0fc857e6da8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$110bn funding round</a><span> that would value firm at $840bn</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>ChatGPT ads expand as </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/125d229d-bfd8-4da0-bc89-1ddd06fced2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more brands and trigger patterns</a><span> emerge.</span></p></li></ul>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AI song generator startups Suno and Udio </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f8239a0d-749c-440f-aa87-ca9ee0ef39a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">angered</a><span> the music industry. <li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>How tech </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a683c8fe-724c-4991-84fe-599f8cb48e37?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">turned against</a><span> women.</span></p></li>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok returns to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/20ce845d-d93b-46a8-b868-4876d548d649?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Albania</a><span> after government-imposed ban.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dc16a6f0-b0fb-4275-a2a9-22d01de2ab16?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">MLB</a><span> gives TikTok creators access to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/50188039-5002-4924-860d-6308ea2d2480?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">content library </a><span>in expanded partnership.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Kentucky TikTok lawsuit calling </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3412df7c-3158-4913-9c22-2e4cbbe96dd6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">app harmful</a><span> to teens can </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/01185ecf-31c0-4b82-a073-4701e7c9f528?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">move forward</a><span>, judge rules.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/78be5770-6a6b-4d32-bcaf-a6775b4014fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">UGC</a><span> has lapped TikTok in influencer marketing, and Instagram is next.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>A study of 406 TikTok users found that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/48820d69-b754-4ce8-be11-589edeec2252?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">97% of them have followed a recipe</a><span> they found on the app.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/33a8423c-971d-457b-8afb-b5d8f1093bcc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok’s true ad impact</a><span> is a slow burn, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cedb7e88-b31b-441b-8fea-2f9c98df3c2f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">incrementality research</a><span> shows.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Haus found that the first half of a given campaign period frequently showed negligible differences between exposed and unexposed consumer groups. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/03089971-f8ee-4f17-a53a-4d7faec70e8a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Measurable impact</a><span> began emerging in the second half of the campaign window.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Did Romania’s ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4142e0fd-94c7-4152-8b62-c0c27115457c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">King of TikTok’</a><span> really sway a presidential election?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Seedance 2.0 might be </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/326604f2-b771-405a-94aa-7f81ed3ee672?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">gen AI video’s next big hope</a><span>, but it’s still slop.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>ByteDance valued at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7dc5337b-7d10-422a-97e5-681abc42af8e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$550 billion</a><span> in proposed share sale by General Atlantic.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Netflix’s ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1fd79cf5-af9a-4efc-ba75-b534e5622c58?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ANTM</a><span>’ doc is fueling a viral photoshoot trend.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9d5b3865-3ea8-4794-b3a4-d54ade388b99?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">releases</a><span> its 2026 Discover list to spotlight key creators in different categories. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/287c7e29-5ea6-4cab-9a16-0a4aea478995?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok</a><span> craze drives ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/40b239ef-9bd5-43a5-8e1c-a5f406191b12?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">all-time record demand</a><span>’ for dairy product.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Capturing the nine-to-five: why ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a7381d3e-5536-4e9f-a0b2-d1952aaef3b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">WorkTok</a><span>’ films are gripping Gen Z.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🫒 Report Break: Nearly </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/44387d1d-496c-4ead-af64-7743b1e26bcb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">one in two consumers (49%) </a><span>now use TikTok as a search engine.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">38% of business owners use TikTok influencers for product sales or promotions. Business owners said their biggest challenge is converting TikTok engagement into sales (38%), followed by increasing follower counts and engagement rates (36%).</p>
</blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f97ab05b-5cb4-4aad-a828-74cf9e765ec5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram</a><span> faces creator anger after AI shopping test.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/027e1782-dde4-48b6-9f16-bac2dfd65932?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">I spent all my time on Instagram</a><span>,’ says woman in landmark trial.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta users survey found </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0e58cc6b-8b42-49a7-b8b3-ffce1b4c1f23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">19% of young teens</a><span> on Instagram report seeing unwanted nude images.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram head pressed on</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8c6f3e98-bdcc-4d5d-b33f-6893994de8c4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> lengthy delay to launch teen safety features</a><span>, like a nudity filter, court filing reveals.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3178323d-83a0-448b-a40a-bb0db7c594e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">testing a shortcut</a><span> to quickly start DM conversations.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram app launches on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/99db7698-8c1d-462b-8258-b79b1308c8b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google TV</a><span> with Reels on the big screen.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8520fc9f-2ab4-41de-8b68-a911262f1d30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram</a><span> expands access to creator tools and provides a clearer eligibility overview.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>DMs 2.0: Threads to use </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1423682b-864c-4a09-8d3f-a039db4946fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a larger font for posts</a><span> that contain only a single emoji.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span data-component-name="MentionStatic" style="white-space: nowrap;cursor: pointer;color: #e85c57;border-radius: 4px;transition: color .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),background-color .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),box-shadow .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),border .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),border-radius .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),opacity .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),filter .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),stroke .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),transform .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),scale .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),outline .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1);margin: 0 -.125em;padding: 0 .125em .0625em;text-decoration: none;"><a href="https://open.substack.com/users/8247620-rachel-karten?utm\_source=mentions" style="-webkit-text-decoration: none;color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Rachel Karten</a></span><span> found Instagram’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1ddef4bb-5f35-4656-9f2f-5cd888f1884a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">finsta</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram working on a “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/83df9567-61f7-48e8-848d-203d9a03e8b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Story Spotlight</a><span>” feature for the upcoming paid subscription. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram is testing a new Home feed section to highlight </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e2c52382-0946-4628-8252-b4b18015e92b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">expiring stories</a><span>. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Disability charities warn of “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b55de421-5fc3-43a9-aac8-49dc2fe712db?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fetishising</a><span>” AI profiles on social media.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram Search testing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ff2d95bf-73e0-4fbf-86a2-3d54c93cf1a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a new layout</a><span> for video results.</span></p></li>
</ul>
